4Q26/2027 年獲利將續創新高，受惠 DDR4 供不應求延續 
預估 4Q26 營收季增 13.2%至 1,512.46 億 元 ， 其 中 預 期ASP 再 季 增
15~20%、雖收斂但仍維持雙位數季增；毛利率季增 0.4ppts 至 86.1%，報
價維持高檔下與 3Q26 相近；EPS 季增 11.9%至 28.42 元。展望後續，月產
能未來四年內將由目前約 65kwpm 分階段新增 35.9kwpm 的產能、2H27 開
始貢獻，供需緊俏延續下 ASP 將維持高檔，4Q26/2027 年營運續創新高。 
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，最主要驅動
力 Server 於整體位元需求佔比過半且供需差達 20%以上(高於整體 DRAM
的約 10%)，其中預期 2027 年 HBM 市場規模年增 243%，Nvidia VR200 搭
載 SOCAMM2 位元需求成長達 6 倍，General Server 亦佔整體 Server 需求
一半比重，相關 RDIMM、LPDRAM 成長強勁；同時新增產能相對需求成長
仍有限，三大原廠 2027 年底 DRAM 投片月產能僅年增 10~15%，中國同業
長鑫存儲雖擴產積極仍僅佔整體產能約 15%，主要供應中國國內需求。此外，
DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 仍具龐大需求，目前 AI Server 仍有約 10%週
邊應用搭載 DDR4，供給則在國際原廠 EOL 下大幅縮減，長鑫存儲重心亦
已轉往 DDR5，預估 2027 年 DDR4 仍有 20%以上的供需缺口(詳見圖 7)。 
產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運展望正向 
綜 合 上 述 ， 預 估2026/27 年 營 收 年 增 525.5%/66.8% 至 4,165.31 億元
/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5% ，EPS 年增
3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。考量 1) 公司現為 DDR4 第一大廠且同
時具備 DDR5 產品布局、2) 產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升、3) WoW 客製
化產品有望帶來更穩定獲利支撐，中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB(過去
三年落在 0.4~2.8 倍)、2027 年 BVPS 2 30.7 元，給予買進、目標價 570 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
4Q26 預期 ASP 再季增 15~20%，2027 年 DDR4 供不應求延續下合約
價仍維持高檔，獲利將年增 68.6%續創新高。 
⧫  
2027 年 DRAM 產業 供 需 緊 俏 及 報 價 上 行 持 續 ， 最 主 要 驅 動 力 為
Server 相關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長，新增產能則相對有限。 
⧫  
產品組合續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運更加穩健，給予買進、目標
價 570 元(2.5x 2027 年 BVPS)。

營運分析
3Q26 獲利季增 74.6%，主因 ASP 季增>60%
3Q26 營收 1,336.49 億元，季增 61.9%/年增 611.7%，其中 1) 平均售價(ASP)：季增超過 60%/年
增超過 500%， 受 惠 半 年 期/一年期等 LTA 重 新 議 價 及 季 度 合 約 價 調 整 、2) 位 元 出 貨 量(Bit
shipment)：季減中個位數百分比 /年減低個位數百分比，主因新廠產能尚未開出且庫存處於低水位；
毛利率達 85.7%，季增 6.2ppts/年增 67.2ppts，受惠 ASP 大幅成長；營業利益率 80%，季增
6.3ppts/年增 74ppts，費用方面提列當期及未來之員工薪酬增加；稅後獲利 876.27 億元，季增
74.6%/年增 5505.1%；EPS 25.40 元，整體 3Q26 獲利大致符合市場預期。

4Q26 DDR4 供需持續緊俏，ASP 預估仍延續雙位數季增幅但收斂至 15~20%
展望 4Q26，預估營收季增 13.2%/年增 402.6%至 1,512.46 億元，其中  1) ASP ：預估季增
15~20%，考量 DDR4 供需緊俏延續，雖季增幅收斂且本季較無如 3Q26 LTA 重新議價的挹注，但
仍預估季增雙位數百分比、2) 位元出貨量：考量 5A 新廠產能尚待 2H27 起陸續開出，此前僅能以
生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻，且庫存仍處低水位，預估季 減低個位數百分比；毛利率
季增 0.4ppts/年增 37ppts 至 86.1%，預期在報價維持高檔下毛利率與 3Q26 水準相近；營業利益
率季增 0.4ppts/年增 41.2ppts 至 80.4%；稅後獲利季增 11.9%/年增 784%至 980.62 億元；EPS

28.42 元續創新高。

供不應求延續下，2027 年 DRAM 合約價仍維持高檔且獲利續創新高
本次法說會重點包括：1) 公司表示 DDR4 市場環境健康，DDR4 目前佔整體 DRAM 市場需求仍有
約 12~14%，雖需求逐漸縮小但對應大幅縮減的供給仍相當強勁，ASP 仍有望保持雙位數成長；2)
2026 年資本支出指引不超過新台幣 697 億元，2026~2029 年 5A 新廠資本支出 預算約新台幣
3,466 億元，屏東新廠長期資本支出規劃達 100 億美元，主要用於 AI 應用的 WoW 3D 晶圓封裝與

測試需求，預計 2H28 起設備裝機； 3) Server/AI 相關目前營收占比 >20%，公司指引將成長至
2029~2030 年的 40~50%，預期隨新產能上線持續提升。
展望 2026/2027 年，目前月產能推估約 65kwpm，而 5A 新廠具備約 50kwpm 的擴充空間，目前規
劃 2028 年將投產至 30kwpm，新增產能預計於 2H27 開始微幅貢獻，相關資本支出預算約新台幣
3,466 億元(含 EUV 設備)，長期而言公司亦預計於屏東科學園區投資設立 3D 晶圓封裝測試廠，導
入矽穿孔(TSV)、晶圓堆疊等 3DIC 先進技術；位元出貨量方面，2026 年在晶圓產出尚待新廠產能
開出、以生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻下，公司維持年增 high-10s % 的指引，2027 年
考量高基期且新廠產能於下半年才開始貢獻，預估 2H27 前位元出貨量維持平穩。
ASP 則受惠市場供需緊俏，1~3Q26 每季皆季增約 60~70%，4Q26 估亦將維持 15~20%的雙位數

季增幅；進入 2027 年，DDR4 供不應求態勢延續，但考量報價已處高檔、非 AI 下游終端漸難以負
荷，且記憶體原廠毛利率多已達 80%以上，預估 2027 年各季 ASP 持平至季增個位數百分比。
綜合上述，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元；毛利率年
增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，2026 年毛利率受惠 ASP 大幅年增已顯著提升至高水位，2027
年報價上行趨勢持續但逐漸持穩於高檔，折舊方面， 2026 年公司預估資本支出以不超過 新台幣
697 億元為上限，其中約 50%用於設備採購，2027 年隨 5A 新廠投產所需設備投資持續增加，資
本支出將大幅年增，故在 2026 年隨先前廠房設備折舊陸續到期使折舊成本相對低之後，2027 年折
舊金額將明顯增加，但在目前 ASP 與獲利皆維持高檔且加計新產出貢獻下，整體 2027 年毛利率受
折舊影響可控；營益率年增 68.8ppts/2.2ppts 至 76.7%/78.9%；稅後獲利年增 3861%/68.6%至

2,619.4 億元/4,416.29 億元；EPS 75.93/128.01 元。
除報價與產能拉升外，中長期可關注趨勢包括： 1) Server/AI 相關應用占比持續提升、 2) DDR5
16Gb 6400/7200Mbps 及以上貢獻增加、3) WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體放量。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第5 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析 
受惠 DDR4 供需緊俏及寡佔優勢、Server/AI 占比續增，亦具備 DDR5 產品布局 
雖三大國際原廠 DDR4 EOL 以將產能轉向 HBM/DDR5，中國同業長鑫存儲(CXMT)亦將重心轉往
DDR5，但 DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 等仍具龐大需求，在供給大幅縮減下，公司目前已為全球
第一大 DDR4 供應商，高度寡占且具備價格主導權，除 8Gb DDR4 以外，16Gb 亦於 2H26 開始貢
獻營收。公司目前 Server/AI 相關應用營收比重已超過 20%，且占比將隨新產能開出而提升，持續
受惠 Storage、BMC、NIC、Switch 應用等貢獻，相較國內同業有望減緩消費性疲弱影響。 
DDR4 為公司目前產業地位、獲利最有利的產品組合，但其亦具備 DDR5 技術能力與相關營收貢獻，
目前採 1B 製程的 16Gb DDR5 已量產出貨，傳輸速率現以 6400Mbps 為主並持續開發 7200Mbps
及以上規格。未來若終端客戶需求加速由 DDR4 轉向 DDR5，公司得以靈活調整產能配置以增加出
貨，持續拿下市場份額。除一般型 DRAM 以外，公司亦有 LPDDR4 產品出貨，LPDDR5 則有望於
2027 年導入量產。 
圖 8：目前營收仍以 DDR4 為主

圖 9：1B/1C 營收占比持續提升

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體為長期成長利基，布局 Edge AI 與外溢市場需求 
WoW(Wafer-on-Wafer)客製化高頻寬記憶體為有利公司中長期營運體質的重要成長動能。目前的標
準 HBM 為 IC 對 IC 堆疊，主要用於 Cloud AI 訓練等市場，然而 Edge 端裝置受限於功耗、成本與
物理體積等限制無法直接採用，同時三大國際原廠有限之 HBM 產能亦無法完全滿足整體 Cloud 端
市場需求，公司亦將受惠此外溢效果。其客製化產品採用 Wafer-on-Wafer Hybrid Bonding( 晶圓級
混合鍵合)技術，將 DRAM 與邏輯 Core Die 直接對接堆疊，能以顯著之成本與功耗優勢滿足客戶於
Edge 端的高頻寬需求。目前有多個客戶專案進行中，預估 2H27~2028 年可見較清晰的量產營收，
新廠產能規劃上亦保留彈性予 WoW 客製化 DRAM 專案，長線有望為公司提供較穩定的獲利支撐。 
圖 10：Wafer-on-wafer 堆疊技術示意圖

資料來源：Samsung、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第6 頁，共 20頁  
財務分析 
整體財務結構於產業上行及下行循環期間皆維持穩健 
公司在 2020~2022 年 上 行 循 環 期 間 ， 流 動 比 率 與 速 動 比 率 分 別 高 達2020/2021/2022 年的
874%/678%/652%及 707%/599%/502%，後續下行期間雖降至 2023/2024 年的 480%/329%及
339%/217%，但整體仍位於健康水準，且負債比率在上行及下行期間均維持在低於 20%的低水準，
短長期償債能力無虞。2025 年以來隨著記憶體市況轉佳、報價顯著上漲，加上 1H26 完成 787.18
億元之私募案，流動比率與速動比率再回升至 2Q26 的 691%與 635%，負債比率則為 17%，預期
後續持續優化。 
經營能力方面，應收/應付帳款週轉天數落在 36~60 天/19~48 天的健康區間，且兩者差距在合理範
圍內。然而，存貨週轉天數受記憶體景氣循環影響，由上行循環期間 2020/2021 年的 130/97 天，
攀升至下行循環期間 2023/2024 年的 271/333 天，使整體現金轉換週期由 2021 年的 120 天拉長至
2024 年 337 天，反映景氣谷底期間庫存積壓對營運資金運轉效率帶來短期壓力。不過隨著 2025
年以來 DRAM 報價顯著上漲且公司庫存去化加速，2Q26 存貨週轉天數已降至 124 天，帶動現金轉
換週期縮短至 118 天，預期將持續改善。 
觀察現金流與資本支出動向，公司於 2021 年達到 328.47 億元的自由現金流高峰，隨後於
2022~2024 年下行期間，公司雖降低但仍投入 100 多億元的資本支出以推動製程技術組合轉往
10nm 級發展，自由現金流 2023/2024 年轉為淨流出 193.4/141.91 億元。儘管自由現金流短暫承壓，
公司在手現金仍穩健維持於 500 億元以上之充沛水準， 2025 年進入上行循環後營運顯著轉佳，
1H26 自由現金流已達 805.52 億元，加上引進 Kioxia、SanDisk、Solidigm 及 Cisco 之高達 787.18
億元的私募案挹注，使 2Q26 底在手現金大幅提升至 2,176.03 億元的歷史新高，且隨著營運成長
將持續增加，足以支應 5A 新廠未來四年高達 3,466 億元之資本支出規劃，整體財務結構穩健。

產業概況  
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 需求驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 將持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，其中最主要驅動力為 Server 相
關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長且優於預期，Server 佔整體 DRAM 位元需求比重持續增加，預期自
2026 年的 54%成長至 2027 年的 63%，供需差更達 20%以上，高於整體 DRAM 的約 10%，主因：
1) Server 需求大幅成長，如本中心預估 2026/2027 年 ASIC 晶片量年增 91%/88%；2) Server 單位
DRAM Content 持續提升；3) AI Inference/Agentic AI 帶動 DRAM 儲存需求多樣化，如擴大導入
LPDDR(SOCAMM)於 Server 架構中，其中預估 2027 年 Nvidia VR200 機櫃對應 CPU 搭配的
SOCAMM2 位元需求成長高達 6 倍。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第8 頁，共 20頁  
預期 2027 年 HBM 整體市場規模年增 243%，其中位元出貨量年增 55%、ASP 翻倍成長，主因：1) 
HBM 先前因年度議價機制無法反映 2H25 以來 DRAM 漲勢，近期已重新議定 2027 年度合約價；2) 
HBM4 放量出貨，其中 HBM4 佔整體 HBM 產值比重將達 46%。此外，雖 Rubin Ultra 、TPU v9 搭
配的 HBM4E 將由 12Hi 降至 8Hi，但主要為因應供給不足下為確保出貨顆數的應對措施，並非需求
下修。2027 年 HBM 市場份額預期 SK Hynix 約 40~45%、Samsung 約 35~40%、Micron 約
25~30%，SK Hynix 仍為 HBM 龍頭，但因 Samsung HBM4 進度優於同業、SK Hynix 持續調整
HBM4 效能，使 Samsung 市佔率具提升空間。 
圖 21：2027 年 HBM 市場規模將年增 243% 圖 22：HBM 佔整體 DRAM 投片比重持續提升

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
以 BOM Cost 分布來看，DRAM/NAND 漲價對於 General Server 與 PC 價格之影響程度較高，如
DRAM/NAND 漲 10%，對於 AI Server/ General Server/PC/Smartphone 整機價格之影響分別為
1%/5.3%/3~3.6%/2~3%。 
而組裝廠更改記憶體規格之彈性來看，PC 最高、Server 其次、AI Server 接續、Smartphone 最低。
其中 PC 品牌廠因各地所需之規格不同且主機板可調整彈性相對手機高，加上每年都會針對原有的
零組件對供應商要求降價，因此可以最快調整其記憶體設計以因應記憶體價格變化，因此將較快出
現產品降規之情況；Server 部分以 Storage Server 所受之影響最大，尤其對於以中小企業客戶為
主之白牌業者其價格敏感度較高，不過對 CSP 來說對於 General Server/AI Server( 占比小)漲價之
接受度仍高；而 Smartphone 則只能在下一代產品發布前更改設計，多只能自行吸收或是對下游反
映售價，對於品牌廠毛利與終端需求影響較大。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第9 頁，共 20頁  
2027 年原廠 DRAM/NAND 新增產能有限，仍不足以因應強勁成長的需求 
雖 2027 年中部份新產能將上線，三大 DRAM 國際原廠年底投片月產能預估皆僅較 2026 年底增加
10~15%，長鑫存儲擴產幅度雖達 20~30%仍難以滿足中國國內需求 (目前中國 DRAM 自給率僅
30~40%)，即使搭配製程轉換以提升位元產出，仍 無法滿足本中心預估之全球 DRAM 位元需求量
30%以上的強勁成長，其中國際原廠擴產計畫如下： 
1) Samsung ：P4L 自 3Q25 起陸續投產，預計至 2027 年底月產能將達約 150kwpm，主要用於 1c 
DRAM 以因應強勁的 HBM4 需求，並持續推動現有廠房舊製程轉往 1c nm 以滿足 HBM 與通用型
1c DRAM 市場；此外，P5L Fab 1 已於 2025 年底復工、Fab 2 提前至 2026 年 7 月動工，產能目
標高於現有廠房約 50%以上。 
2) SK Hynix ：M15X 2026 下半年預計投產約 40kwpm，2027 年底月產能將達約 80kwpm，主要用
於 1b 並部分導入 1c DRAM ；公司亦表示 Y1 首個無塵室設備裝機將提前 3 個月至 2027 年 5 月，
以因應強勁的 AI 相關記憶體需求，預計至 2030 年上半年分階段開出共 360kwpm 的產能，Y2 則
預計 2027 年 7 月動工、2029 年 6 月投產，用於生產 HBM 及下世代 DRAM。 
3) Micron ：美國 ID1 預計 2027 年中產出第一批晶圓、ID2 則預計 2028 年底產出；向力積電添購
之銅鑼廠預計提前一季至 2027 年中出貨；新加坡廠區 HBM 封裝產能將於 2027 年上半年開始貢獻。 
4) 長鑫存儲：上海新廠 2027 年開始投產，主要針對 HBM3 等高端記憶體產品，亦新建後段封測廠
以填補中國在 TSV 及堆疊封裝的供應鏈空白，合肥 Phase 3 則預計以 LPDDR4X/5/5X 等產品為主，
預計 2028 年開始投產。

獲利預估與股票評價
正向看待南亞科後續營運，目標價 570 元
展望後續，在 ASP 維持高檔且新廠產能將於 2H27 開始逐步貢獻下，正向看待公司中長期營運成
長，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增
60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，EPS 年增 3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。
目前公司股價約在 1.8 倍 2027 年每股淨值(BVPS) ( 隱含本益比 3.2 倍)，低於國外同業平均的 2.0
倍，公司過去三年 PB 則落在 0.4~2.8 倍區間。雖目前循環處於 DRAM 報價仍維持高檔但預期後續
季增幅將收斂的階段，然而考量 1) 公司為 DDR4 第一大廠的產業地位且同時具備 DDR5 產品布局、
2) 產品組合將逐步擺脫消費性並持續往 Server/AI 提升(目前相關營收占比已超過 20%)、3) WoW

客製化產品有望帶來更穩定的獲利支撐，有助公司中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB、2027 年
BVPS 230.7 元，推得目標價 570 元，給予買進評等。

公司簡介  
南亞科技成立於 1995 年，近年於全球 DRAM 市佔率約 1~2%，排名第五，公司目前以 DDR4 為主(佔比 60~70%)，亦具備
DDR5 產品，以應用別而言，PC & Server 約 25~30%、Mobile 約 10~15%、Consumer 約 60~70%。公司生產基地為新北
泰山 3A 廠，月產能 6~6.5 萬片。由於 DDR4/5 為長期主流市場，公司將陸續發展 1B/1C/1D 製程，且預計 2026~2029 年
投入新台幣 3,466 億元建置 5A 新廠產能，預估新產能將於 2H27~2028 年開出。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南亞科整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略為領先的位
置，但在半導體設計與製造的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南亞科的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準相當。公司較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含商業道德以及人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南亞科在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬較佳。ESG 報告體系完善，公
司已指定董事會成員負責監督 ESG 相關事宜。使用 ESG 目標評估高階主管績效；然而，這些目標並未在薪酬政策中明
確規定。儘管如此，公司的檢舉人保護計畫和環境政策均較為完善。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

Idea
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2H26 Driven by Semis and
Photonics; 2027 Seen as
Another Strong Year
A unique combination of power, electrical and optical testing
tool capabilities position the company well in AI deployment.
Stay OW.
We see 3Q26 EPS of NT$13.71: Chroma's 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$17,926mn,
up 32% QoQ and 180% YoY. We believe the strength was driven mainly by the
semiconductor and photonics businesses, while the power testing business likely

remained at an elevated level. Within the semiconductor and photonics businesses,
SL T  should benefit from AI HPC customers' ramping; meanwhile, optical testing
tools for transceivers and, to a lesser extent, CPO, are also seen as growth drivers
during the quarter. We estimate GM of 60.5% (flat QoQ) as scale tailwinds should
be largely offset by a less favorable product mix, while OPM should expand by
0.5ppt QoQ to 39.3% on some operating leverage. Overall, we expect EPS of NT

$13.71 (+13% QoQ/+15% YoY).
Growth momentum seen sustained into 2027: Looking ahead, we expect Chroma
to see another year of strong growth, supported by the power testing (51% growth
for 42% mix) and semi & photonics businesses (58% growth for 40% mix).
• Power testing: The ever-increasing power consumption of AI clusters should
continue to drive demand for higher spec testing tools. Along with our
forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit demand in the next

three years (see report), we think this should stay a major driver for Chroma.
• SLT: We are seeing more HPC chip designers adopting the system level
testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. As the first mover in this market. Chroma should benefit from
increasing chip volume as well as higher TDP, in our view.
• Photonics: Transceivers should continue to be the mainstream solution;
architectures such as CPO and NPO also need testing tools.

• Emerging business: We expect Chroma, by leveraging its existing customer
relationship and technology expertise, to gain more traction in FT handlers
and burn-in ovens for AI HPCs in 2027. These are the markets that Chroma
previously focused less on, so they represent new opportunities.
Stay OW: We remain constructive on Chroma for its testing equipment's unique
combination of power, electrical and optical capabilities, allowing it to capture
growth opportunities in various aspects of AI deployment. Our PT remains NT

$2,800 as our estimate increases are offset by a lower target multiple of 40x 2027e
P/E (previously 45x) to factor in generally more conservative sentiment toward the
AI space now. This implies a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 3: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 4: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 5: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Exhibit 7: Chroma's FT Handler Offering
Source: Chroma
129% YoY growth  seen for the semiconductor and photonics business in
2026
Overall, we forecast semiconductor and photonics revenue will grow  129%  YoY in 2026,
accounting for 36% of Chroma's mix.

### Page 10

Idea
10
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Our price target (base case scenario value) remains  NT$2,800: Our PT is based on a
target multiple of 40x 2027e P/E (vs. 45x 2027e previously).   We lowered our multiple to
factor in recent volatility in market sentiment toward AI.   We view our multiple as justified
based on our estimated 68% operating profit CAGR over 2025-28. This implies a PEG of
0.6 vs. its average of 1.0 in the past decade, when the stock was trading at an average of

~19x forward P/E with an operating profit CAGR of 20%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,900 (unchanged): We apply a 55x multiple (vs. 62x P/E
previously)  to our earnings estimate for 2027.  This scenario reflects stronger  5G and HPC
demand, driving more earnings upside.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (unchanged): We apply a 16x P/E multiple (vs. 18x
P/E previously) to our earnings estimate for 2027. This scenario factors in weaker HPC
revenue as hyperscalers start to focus more on ROI for AI capex than they did in the past

couple of years.
Risks:
• Softer-than-expected AI capex
• Slower progress in advanced packaging capacity expansion
• Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand

Risk Reward - Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
NT$2,800.00
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it
better captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average
valuation of ~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating proﬁt CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG
of 1.0. We think our target multiple of 40x is justiﬁed by the 68% operating proﬁt CAGR we
project for 2025-28.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

 Update
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
  CPO Revenue on Track; Stay 
OW 
 CPO is on track for >US$500mn in 2027: Management mentioned the CPO has 
already started mass production  and will reach at least US$500mn in 2027. This is 
in-line with our estimates that we forecast CPO revenue to be >US$600mn in 2027.
Slightly upward revision of LEAP revenue: The company slightly raised 2026 LEAP 
(leading-edge advanced packaging) revenue from US$3.7bn to US$4bn and  2027 
from >US$7.5bn to >US$8bn, on track  to our forecast of US$9bn. It expects at least 
US$1bn of full process CoWoS-like revenue in 2027 vs. MSe of US$1.8bn.
Margin expansion is also on track: 3Q26 ATM gross margin has already surpassed 
the structural range and reached 30.6%. The company mentioned 30% would be 
the new floor and expects it to continue to expand sequentially in 2027.
3Q26 GM and 4Q26 guidance slightly beat: 3Q26 GM of 22.1% is slightly higher 
than both MSe and the consensus. 4Q26 revenue guidance of 6-8% QoQ growth 
and GM of 23-24% are both slightly higher than MSe of 5% QoQ growth and 22-23% 
GM forecast.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd. (3711.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity of 9.8%, intermediate
growth rate of 10%, terminal growth rate of 3.5%, and cash dividend payout ratio of 60%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected global economic and semiconductor growth
n Margin expansion through merger consolidation
n New wearables project wins with higher margins
Risks to Downside

n Slower-than-expected global economic and semiconductor growth
n Further margin erosion amid price competition
n Lack of new features in wearables, causing consumers to lose interest

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 186: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리_0a1416f9

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리_0a1416f9.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

## Document 224: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다. 
목표주가 (하향) 550,000원  
현재가 (10/28) 285,500원

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일

## Document 254: AWS收入劲增39%但AI支出吓退市场，亚马逊Q3自由现金流恶化，线上销售乏力，盘后跳水｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/AWS收入劲增39%但AI支出吓退市场，亚马逊Q3自由现金流恶化，线上销售乏力，盘后跳水｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Q3 亚⻢逊营收同⽐增 12% 、略低于分析师预期， EPS 同⽐不增反降 3% ，经营利润率逼近
14% 创历史新⾼； AWS 收⼊增速近五年最⾼、预计明后年均增超 40% ； Q3 线上销售仅增
2% 、增速三年半最低，⼴告收⼊较预期低 6% ；资本⽀出翻⼀倍多⾄ 773 亿美元，较预期⾼
逾 30% ；⾃由现⾦流负 424 亿美元、为 Q2 的 5.6 倍； Q4 营收和利润指引中值低于预期。公
司维持今年资本⽀出指引 2200 亿美元不变。股价盘后⼀度跌超 10% 。
摘要：
亚⻢逊三季度财报呈现明显的两⾯性：核⼼经营利润强于预期、备受关注的云计算业务 AWS 保
持⾼速增⻓，但核⼼业务电商增⻓乏⼒，公司总营收和 EPS 盈利均逊于华尔街预期，且 AI 相关资
本开⽀继续维持⾼位，令市场对⾃由现⾦流和投资回报周期的担忧升温。
美东时间 29 ⽇周四美股盘后，亚⻢逊公布，截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的今年第三季度，公司净销售
额同⽐增⻓ 12% ⾄ 2019.4 亿美元，略低于分析师预期的约 2022.6 亿美元；稀释后每股收益

（ EPS ）同⽐下降约 3.1% ⾄ 1.89 美元，分析师预期同⽐微增 0.5% ⾄ 1.96 美元；⽽经营利润同⽐
增 61.5% ⾄ 280.8 亿美元，较市场预期⾼逾 7% ，经营利润率升⾄ 13.9% ，为公司史上最⾼⽔平。
财报最⼤亮点仍是 AWS 。三季度 AWS 净销售额同⽐增⻓ 39.3% ⾄ 459.8 亿美元，较分析师预期⾼
1.3% ，创将近五年来最⾼单季增速，季末年化收⼊运转率达 1840 亿美元，环⽐⼆季度末时增⻓
8.9% 。 CEO Andy Jassy 在财报中强调， AWS 在功能和基础设施上的投⼊正在奏效，并预计
2027 年和 2028 年 AWS 收⼊增速都将超过 40% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

相⽐ AWS ，三季度线上销售仅同⽐增⻓ 2% ，创 2023 年初以来最低单季增速。亚⻢逊对假⽇季、
第四季度销售的指引中值逊于分析师预期也显⽰，其线上业务短期内恐难迅速回暖。
虽然 AWS 收⼊继续劲增，但市场更在意 “ 代价 ” 。亚⻢逊三季度购置物业和设备的资本⽀出⾼达
772.6 亿美元、约为去年同期的 1.2 倍，较市场预期⾼逾 34% ；⾃由现⾦流连续第⼆季录得负值，
从去年同期的流⼊ 147.9 亿美元转为流出 423.6 亿美元，流出规模远超⼆季度。公司还维持约
2200 亿美元的 2026 年全年现⾦资本⽀出指引不变，并未释放 AI 投⼊放缓的信号。
财报公布后，周四本已收跌超 4% 的亚⻢逊股价盘后进⼀步跳⽔，盘后跌幅⼀度超过 10% 。
营收略低于预期，但经营利润率创历史新⾼
从整体收⼊看，亚⻢逊三季度净销售额 2019.4 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 12% ；若剔除汇率影响，增速
为 13% 。
亚⻢逊特别提⽰，今年会员⽇ Prime Day 在公司的多数重要市场地区均提前⾄⼆季度，⽽ 2025 年

Prime Day 完全落在三季度，因此今年三季度同⽐增速受到时点错位影响；若剔除这⼀因素，三
季度收⼊同⽐增速将⾼出约 400 个基点。
利润端则明显更强。亚⻢逊三季度经营利润为 280.8 亿美元，较去年同期的 174.2 亿美元⼤增
61% ，经营利润率从去年同期的 9.7% 提升⾄ 13.9% 。这意味着，即便收⼊略低于预期，公司在
履约、销售营销、管理费⽤等⽅⾯的效率改善，仍然⽀撑了创纪录的经营利润率。

### Page 3

净利润为 206.9 亿美元，低于去年同期的 211.9 亿美元；稀释后 EPS 为 1.89 美元，同⽐下降 3% 。
这 ⼀ 同 ⽐ 下 滑 很 ⼤ 程 度 上 与 去 年 同 期 的 ⾮ 经 营 性 ⾼ 基数 有 关 ——2025 年 三 季 度 亚 ⻢ 逊 因
Anthropic 投资确认了 95 亿美元税前收益，⽽今年同期没有类似收益，其他收⼊ / 费⽤项⽬从去年
同期的正 101.9 亿美元转为负 7 亿美元。
换⾔之，这份财报不是经营层⾯的利润失速，⽽是收⼊和每股收益略低于预期、叠加⾮经营性
项⽬和资本开⽀压⼒，削弱了投资者对短期回报的信⼼。
AWS 继续加速： AI 需求⽀撑云业务，但市场也在追问投⼊回报
AWS 是三季度最亮眼的业务线。该部⻔净销售额 459.8 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 39% ，⾼于分析师预期
的约 454 亿美元，同⽐增速较⼆季度的约 37% 进⼀步加快，继续创 2021 年四季度以来最⾼增速；
AWS 经营利润 177.7 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 55% ，经营利润率为 38.7% ，⾼于去年同期的 34.6% 。

从收⼊结构看，三季度线上销售额 685 亿美元，贡献亚⻢逊三分之⼀的净销售额， AWS 销售额占
总额的 23% ，⾼于去年同期的 18% ，但其贡献了亚⻢逊整体经营利润的约 63% 。这说明亚⻢逊
的利润池仍⾼度依赖云业务，⽽ AWS 的重新加速，对⽀撑集团估值⾄关重要。
财报披露， AWS 近期与 Meta 、英伟达、 OpenAI 、 Salesforce 、甲⻣⽂、⾼通、 Verizon 等签署
了新协议。 Amazon Bedrock 继续快速增⻓，财富 500 强企业在 Bedrock 上的⽀出同⽐接近 7 倍，
整体 token 量同⽐增⻓近 17 倍； Bedrock AgentCore 已有数万名客⼾使⽤，三季度部署的智能体
数量超过前三个季度总和的两倍。
Jassy 对 AWS 前景的表态也⾮常激进。他称 AWS 三季度年化收⼊运⾏率已达 1840 亿美元，并预
计 2027 年和 2028 年 AWS 收⼊增速均超过 40% 。他还表⽰，历史上从未出现过万亿美元收⼊规模

### Page 4

的企业，⽽亚⻢逊未来 5 到 10 年可能拥有两个这样的业务。
但这也是市场分歧所在： AWS 的增⻓说明 AI 投⼊正在转化为需求，但⾼增⻓同时需要巨额算
⼒、数据中⼼和芯⽚投资。投资者并⾮否认 AWS 复苏，⽽是在重新评估这种增⻓需要消耗多少
现⾦流。
AI 资本开⽀成为股价压⼒核⼼：⾃由现⾦流⼤幅转负
资本开⽀是这份财报引发市场负⾯反应的关键。
三季度，亚⻢逊购置物业和设备⽀出为 772.6 亿美元，远⾼于去年同期的 351 亿美元，远超市场
预期的 574.7 亿美元。过去 12 个⽉，公司购置物业和设备净额达到 2106.6 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
82% 。亚⻢逊明确表⽰，这⼀增⻓主要反映了⼈⼯智能相关投资。
现⾦流表显⽰，过去 12 个⽉亚⻢逊经营现⾦流为 1683 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 29% ；但⾃由现⾦流为
流出 423.6 亿美元、流出规模约为⼆季度 76 亿美元的 5.6 倍，⽽去年同期为流⼊ 147.9 亿美元。也
就是说，公司主营业务造⾎能⼒仍在增强，但 AI 基础设施投⼊的速度更快，已经吞噬并反转了⾃

由现⾦流。
资产负债表也体现出扩张压⼒。截⾄三季度末，亚⻢逊现⾦及现⾦等价物为 557.8 亿美元，低于
去年底的 868.1 亿美元；⻓期债务升⾄ 1731.3 亿美元，⽽去年底为 656.5 亿美元。三季度公司新
增⻓期债务融资 481 亿美元，前三季度新增⻓期债务融资 1151 亿美元。
对成⻓型科技公司⽽⾔，资本开⽀本⾝并⾮负⾯，尤其是在 AWS 增速重新上⾏的背景下。但当
三季度资本开⽀⼤幅超预期、⾃由现⾦流显著转负，同时公司⼜维持全年约 2200 亿美元现⾦资
本开⽀预期时，市场⾃然会担⼼： AI 投⼊何时进⼊回报期？未来⼏年是否还会持续挤压现⾦流？
北美和国际业务：收⼊受 Prime Day 错位影响，利润改善明显
零售相关业务表⾯上增速不强，但利润端改善较为明显。
北美业务三季度净销售额 1128.5 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 6% ，略低于市场预期的约 1133.9 亿美元；经
营利润 87.2 亿美元，较去年同期的 47.9 亿美元⼤增 82% ，经营利润率从 4.5% 提升⾄ 7.7% 。

国际业务净销售额 431.1 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 5% ，也略低于预期；若剔除汇率影响，增速为 8% 。
该部⻔经营利润 15.8 亿美元，⾼于去年同期的 12 亿美元，经营利润率从 2.9% 提升⾄ 3.7% 。

### Page 5

亚⻢逊解释称， Prime Day 时点变化压低了三季度收⼊增速。在线商店收⼊ 684.7 亿美元，同⽐
仅增⻓ 2% ；第三⽅卖家服务收⼊ 464.9 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 9% ；实体店收⼊ 57.1 亿美元，同⽐增
⻓ 2% 。全球付费商品数量同⽐增⻓ 12% ，全球运输成本同⽐增⻓ 11% ，显⽰订单量仍在增⻓，
但收⼊结构受到促销时点影响。
因此，零售业务不能简单理解为需求恶化。更准确地说，三季度零售收⼊表现不如市场预期，
但利润率改善和配送效率提升仍然延续。公司称， Prime 会员三季度收货速度创历史同期最快，
超过 15 亿件商品实现当⽇或次⽇送达。
⼴告业务增⻓放缓，是财报中另⼀短板
⼴告业务三季度表现低于预期，是除资本开⽀外另⼀个压制情绪的因素。
亚⻢逊⼴告服务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 11% ⾄ 196.4 亿美元，较分析师预期收⼊ 208.7 亿美元低约 6% ；剔
除汇率影响增⻓ 12% 。这⼀增速相⽐此前多个季度 20% 以上的增⻓明显放缓，也低于市场预期
的约 208.7 亿美元。

公司表⽰，⼴告收⼊受到 Prime Day 时点以及其他若⼲时间因素影响，并预计四季度⼴告收⼊同
⽐增速将回升⾄⾼⼗位数区间。如果这⼀指引兑现，三季度⼴告降速可能更多是时点扰动，⽽
⾮⼴告业务结构性放缓。
不过，市场短期难免会更敏感。⼴告是亚⻢逊近年来利润率更⾼、增⻓更快的业务之⼀，⼀旦
增速放缓，就会削弱投资者对零售⽣态商业化能⼒的信⼼。相⽐之下，订阅服务表现较稳，三
季度收⼊ 140.1 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 11% ，⾼于市场预期的约 136.9 亿美元。
四季度指引不算差，但营收和利润中值低于预期
亚⻢逊给出的四季度指引总体处于华尔街预期附近，但并未带来⾜够惊喜。
公司预计四季度净销售额在 2400 亿⾄ 2470 亿美元之间，同⽐增⻓ 12% ⾄ 16% ，其中包含约 60 个
基点的汇率不利影响。该营收区间的中值为 2435 亿美元，略低于分析师预期的 2445.2 亿美元，
但预期值落在指引区间内。
更关键的是经营利润指引。亚⻢逊预计四季度经营利润为 285 亿⾄ 335 亿美元，⽽市场预期约

331 亿美元。换⾔之，公司指引上限仅略⾼于共识预期，中点 310 亿美元则低于预期。
这解释了为何股价反应如此剧烈：三季度营收和 EPS 均⼩幅不及预期，⼴告收⼊低于预期，四
季度利润指引中点偏弱，同时资本开⽀⼤幅超预期且全年 AI 投⼊计划维持⾼位。 AWS 的强劲增

## Document 295: European bank M&A is back with a bang

Source PDF: all report pdf/European bank M&A is back with a bang.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

European bank M&A has seen a resurgence in recent years, and
2027 should stay busy. Domestic deals still dominate, while
cross-border consolidation is harder but is also picking up.
Regulation is becoming more supportive, but politics and
execution still matter. Expect more deals, but a transformation
of Europe's banking sector is still some way oﬀ
Jesse Norcross
UniCredit and Commerzbank are at the centre of Europe’s closely watched
push towards cross-border banking consolidation

European bank M&A is back
European bank M&A has seen a strong resurgence, with several
large-scale deals making headlines. UniCredit’s high-stakes
takeover of Commerzbank, once deemed very unlikely but now
moving closer to completion, could be Europe’s most signiﬁcant
cross-border transaction in years. Italy remains one of Europe’s
bank M&A hotbeds, with multiple banks taking part in the ongoing
consolidation wave. Other banks have also been active, and we

expect M&A to remain elevated in 2027.

### Page 2

Year-to-date M&A deal value has already surpassed the 2025 total,
reaching €53bn including the UniCredit-Commerzbank transaction
and based on deals valued at €100m or more. Furthermore, deals
pending or intended total €39bn, which includes the potential
takeover of Banca Monte dei Paschi by Intesa Sanpaolo, currently
valued at around €34bn. All in, this would bring deal value back to
levels not seen since the early 2010s.
European bank M&A volumes have seen a

resurgence

Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING
The rationale for European bank M&A has changed. After the
ﬁnancial crisis, deals were mostly about repair, followed by a wave
of deals involving stronger domestic banks buying weaker rivals.
Today, the backdrop has shifted – proﬁtability and capital levels are
stronger, making the obvious cost-eﬃciency story less compelling

on its own. Instead, banks are using M&A to add scale, deposits and
fee-generating diversiﬁcation, while the need to close the gap with
US rivals remains a key driver.
The industrial logic has also strengthened, as ﬁxed costs are rising
in areas such as technology, AI, compliance, as well as personnel.
Rising investment requirements mean that remaining subscale is
becoming more expensive, even for highly proﬁtable banks.

### Page 3

Domestic dominates, but cross-border
competes
UniCredit-Commerzbank remains the sector’s most closely
watched deal and could become the ﬁrst major cross-border
acquisition since the ﬁnancial crisis. UniCredit has slowly been
building its stake rather than a clean takeover and is now deemed
to hold eﬀective control, despite currently owning around 50% of
the shares. Furthermore, it is also not entirely a leap into an
unfamiliar market, as UniCredit already has a signiﬁcant banking

business in Germany through its subsidiary, HVB.
The deal has also faced considerable pushback as well as political
and stakeholder opposition, but is now looking increasingly likely to
proceed. It has highlighted the diﬃculty of pulling oﬀ a large-scale
cross-border deal for European banks. Yet it is now becoming the
clearest live test of whether Europe is genuinely ready for cross-
border bank consolidation.
Domestic deals remain the rule, but we have seen other examples

of cross-border deals. These include BPCE’s acquisition of
Portuguese peer Novo Banco; Erste Bank's acquisition of Santander
Bank Polska; and Crédit Agricole acquiring IKB to increase its
presence in Germany. Some banks are also looking across the
pond, such as Santander, which acquired Webster Financial
Corporation in the US for USD12.5bn.
These transactions are important, but they do not yet amount to a
broad-based cross-border consolidation wave. Many involve banks

already familiar with the target market or assets that complement
an existing local business.
Selected transaction details

### Page 4

Source: Company reports, ING
Italy is still riding the wave
Italy has become one of Europe’s busiest M&A markets, riding a
new wave of consolidation, with Banca Monte dei Paschi (MPS) at
the epicentre. The bank completed the acquisition of Mediobanca in
2025, and this year approached Banco BPM and Banca Generali for
a merger as a counteroﬀensive to Intesa Sanpaolo’s hostile bid for
MPS.
The counteroﬀensive now appears less likely, as key shareholders

have opposed parts of MPS’s merger plans and have also backed
Intesa’s bid. The ECB has also provided authorisation to take control
of MPS, representing an important step for the €34bn takeover.
Alongside this, we had the BPER–Popolare di Sondrio merger earlier
in the year, adding to Italy’s consolidation.
Why is Italy so active in particular? Well, Italy remains quite
fragmented (see below) and still oﬀers a large domestic synergy
pool, even after the wave of M&A that has already taken place.

Furthermore, the country has meaningful industrial scale and oﬀers
further revenue diversiﬁcation opportunities for banks and insurers.
Although the obvious combinations are becoming less evident in
Italy, we expect this to continue to drive M&A activity in 2027.

country (2025)

It's not only about getting bigger
The deal mix is also broadening, as banks are increasingly focusing
on fee income as well as scale, while also stepping into capital-light
businesses. Deals in sectors such as wealth management, asset
management, payments & FX and insurance are likely to feature
prominently.
NatWest Group’s acquisition of Evelyn Partners is a good example.

The combination created the UK’s largest Private Banking and
Wealth Management business, while lifting Natwest’s fee income
by 20%. Similarly, BNP Paribas’s acquisition of AXA Investment
Managers in 2025 created a €1.5tn asset management business,
as the bank is seeking to compete with US asset management
rivals and grow its fee income. In Germany, Deutsche Bank and
DWS were reported to be exploring a potential minority investment
in the life insurance business Frankfurter Leben. This would mark

Deutsche’s return to life insurance after more than a decade.
Consolidation can support bank
performance
But does consolidation actually deliver better outcomes? The
evidence is not entirely clear-cut, but overall points in the right
direction. While increasing scale is not always the best use of
capital, in general, more consolidated markets tend to have more
stable and, in some cases, higher proﬁtability. The ECB is in favour
of further consolidation where appropriate, as it sees the beneﬁts of

reduced following a merger. This could be a downside, as better
capitalised banks tend to enjoy lower funding costs and thus have
more room to lend to the real economy.
The outcome therefore depends on the trade-oﬀ between longer-
term synergies and the upfront capital, execution and integration
costs.
ECB data show an association between more concentrated banking
markets and proﬁtability. As the chart below shows, countries with

a high level of concentration (deﬁned by a higher Herﬁndahl-
Hirschman Index coeﬃcient) tend to have higher net interest
margins on average. Still, net interest margins in Europe are
structurally lower than in the US, where they tend to be between 3–
4%.

Similarly, a higher level of market concentration in European

greater concentration can lead to lower asset quality costs,
although the relationship should perhaps not be interpreted as
causal. Still, taken together, this suggests that consolidation can
improve banking markets.

Regulation is helping, but can get in the way
The Banking Union has made deals easier to assess: the ECB has
become the single prudential supervisor for large banks, capital
rules are more consistent, while resolution planning is now much

clearer than it was even a decade ago. But as the Draghi report
noted, the incomplete Banking Union means Europe’s banks remain
less proﬁtable than their US counterparts.
There is a shift underway, though. The EU Commission plans to
table a broad Banking Package in the ﬁrst quarter of 2027, with
measures covering cross-border capital and liquidity management,
deposit insurance, prudential rules and other items. For M&A, the
most important proposals include those to give banking groups

more ﬂexibility to allocate capital and liquidity more eﬃciently
within cross-border groups, while also applying more consistent

treatment to intra-group exposures. These remain proposals rather
than agreed implementations, meaning their ﬁnal scope and
timetable remain uncertain.
The AFME estimates that regulatory ring-fencing rules mean that
some €225bn of capital and €250bn of liquidity is trapped within
subsidiaries, which banks cannot freely move between parent and
subsidiaries. Freeing this up could also support more cross-border
transactions.

The European Commission is also bringing forward a new proposal
to replace the European Deposit Insurance Scheme (EDIS), which
could reduce national ring-fencing incentives and encourage more
cross-border M&A. CMDI reform has been passed this year, which
could make it easier to deal with failing banks, potentially making
consolidation easier as well, although the direct impact on M&A is

unclear.
The Danish compromise, which is now embedded into CRR3, allows
banks to risk-weight certain insurance subsidiaries rather than
deduct them from regulatory capital. This was clariﬁed by the EBA
in January 2026, and some banks such as UniCredit have received
authorisation to use the Danish Compromise methodology for its
insurance subsidiaries. This has improved the economics of

bancassurance and could support further bank-insurance
transactions.
On the other hand, national regulatory and political intervention
has also put the brakes on M&A in some cases, raising execution
costs and uncertainty. BBVA’s proposed acquisition of Sabadell in
2024 illustrates the problem. It took 539 days from the
announcement in May 2024 to the oﬀer failing in October 2025.
For us, the message seems clear that European regulators are
seeking to improve bank competitiveness, and they see

consolidation as an important part of this. Looking ahead,
regulation can improve the plumbing, but it is not a dealmaker.
Why 2027 should stay busy
The starting point for 2027 remains supportive, with bank
proﬁtability continuing to hold up well in 2026 while capital levels
remain solid. While the geopolitical, inﬂation and interest rate
backdrop poses headwinds, we still expect acquisition activity to
remain high. On the other hand, management teams will

increasingly need to choose between retaining greater capital
headroom in a less market-friendly environment, shareholder
payouts, or M&A.
The most likely deals are still domestic. Italy remains the M&A
market of choice, but other geographies could also stand out. The
Nordics are likely to see more partnerships and tech- and wealth-
focused deals. Spain and Portugal are already quite concentrated,
so we could see more bolt-ons. In the UK, challenger banks and

neobanks are gaining ground while deposit competition remains
intense, potentially leading to more activity. The Benelux has
potential catalysts following a series of government ownership
reductions and increasing inorganic ambitions.
Cross-border activity could also remain elevated, but we think the
real litmus test remains the UniCredit-Commerzbank deal, including
what happens after completion in terms of execution and
integration, a process that is likely to take a few years. The

regulatory environment should also become increasingly more

supportive for cross-border M&A from 2027 onwards.
Overall, 2027 could be another interesting year.
M&A is here to stay
European bank M&A has picked up in recent years, and we expect
more activity again in 2027, although we do not see a big bang. For
investors, positioning is inherently speculative. For bondholders, the
main way to gain is by owning the target entity's bonds. However,
this is not always the case, and bigger doesn’t always mean better,

as credit ratings don’t necessarily improve either following an
acquisition or merger.
We are not suggesting that a major change in the European
banking landscape is imminent, as many obstacles remain,
particularly with cross-border deals. But the direction of travel is
interesting: larger banks often outperform smaller banks, while
regulators continue to see beneﬁts from a deeper, more integrated
European banking union. Still, European banking markets remain

fragmented to diﬀering degrees, both between countries and
compared to the US, presenting both opportunities and obstacles.

## Document 298: Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

September 2026: Marked improvement in
services business confidence
September 2026: Business climate in the
manufacturing sector comfortably in the
expansion zone for the 8th month in a row
September 2026: Strengthening inflationary
pressures
September 2026: Slight further increase in

September 2026: First decrease in 5 months of
household confidence
September 2026: Marked increase in inflation,
to a 3-year high
Eurozone: Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer

confidence falls back
02/10/2026 3
We have selected two inflation measures (with and without energy) and six survey indicators: 1/ & 2/ business confidence, as
measured by the PMIs in the manufacturing and services sectors; 3/ & 4/ input and output prices components of the
composite PMI (in order to identify direct inflationary pressures); 5/ the suppliers’ delivery times component of the
manufacturing PMI (a direct indicator of possible supply difficulties and supply-demand imbalance, and therefore, indirectly,

of inflationary pressures in the making); and 6/ consumer confidence (measured by the “assessment of financial situation in
the next 12 months” in order to capture the impact of inflation on purchasing power). The trends in each of these indicators
are observed relative to month m=0, corresponding to the start of the conflict. Each line does not represent the level of the
indicator, but its cumulative variation compared to month m=0.
The overall picture from the confidence surveys available for September is globally positive. According to the PMIs, the

business climate in services has indeed seen a clear improvement, and that in the manufacturing sector remains comfortably
in expansion territory for the 8 th month in a row. However, consumer confidence has recorded its first decline in 5 months,
likely driven by rising inflation and concerns over purchasing power. News on the inflation front is negative. PMI surveys
report a strengthening of inflationary pressures: this trend is not a surprise given the tensions in energy prices, but it is

nonetheless significant. As for inflation itself, it continues to rise, more markedly in September: +0.6 percentage points, to
3.8% year-on-year (Eurostat flash estimate), of which 0.4 pp du to the higher energy contribution. Inflation excluding energy
increased as well, by 0.2 pp to 2.3% (of which 0.1 pp due to the rise in food inflation).
Source: S&P Global, Eurostat, European Commission, Macrobond, BNP Paribas

AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%.

average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.

 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

weighted earnings of $625.1B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q4. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q4. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Industrials sector has 
the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q4. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (145.6%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $59.5B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $24.2B in 25Q4. 3 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (366.3%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (175.7%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 52.1%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (56.3%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $292.1B in 26Q4, compared to earnings of $186.8B in 25Q4. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (119.5%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (72.3%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 19.1%. 
The Industrials sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $52B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $50.1B in 25Q4. 2 of the 20 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower earnings 
than a year ago. The Aerospace & Defense (-39.6%) and Passenger Airlines (-29.8%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 30.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

## Document 350: Open mouth operations (2026-10-31)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Open mouth operations (2026-10-31).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
Open mouth operations
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
13:34 October 23 2026 Share
The RBA does not want to promise what it will do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks.
Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for every nuance. Lately, there seems to have
been more RBA communication to pore over. The method of delivery has also evolved. This has

### Page 2

Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks for clues to future rate moves. They will
have plenty to pore over in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry of fireside chats
this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday.
Next Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before Senate Estimates, just as the
quarterly CPI is released. (This timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After the

decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a media release and press conference. More
speeches will follow, including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just following the RBA Review but also more
recently. There may be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication outside that certainly
feels more frequent than a couple of years ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little

sparse outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary appearances. We have counted
58 public engagements so far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website. This compares
with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in 2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events were related to economics, markets
and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed as well. There are fewer set-piece

speeches on policy and a lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few years ago, there
are more interviews across a wide range of media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It
was a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the 7:30 Report; it is hard to imagine him
agreeing to an Assistant Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general audience. Central banks want to explain

why they do what they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising but the household
sector is not the source of the inflation.
Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief
Communication Officer highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting media conference is
designed to speak to the public. Market participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
sometimes caused confusion when tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different tone and

varying complexity.
The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly using speeches directed to professional
audiences to discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such as neutral rates and
financial conditions. They are also highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more ways
than just squeezing mortgage holders.
One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises

about the future path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running commentary about how it
is interpreting recent data. These interpretations are themselves useful signals about what the RBA
thinks it might do.

### Page 3

the primary audience. So if you think that what the Governor said in the media conference sounded
more ‘dovish’ than what another official says in a more market-oriented forum, it is partly because she
is trying to convey that the RBA understands that its recent policy actions are making things harder for
many Australians. But this is in much the same way that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It
does, but that will not stop the treatment.

A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted material such as the minutes and set-
piece speeches than in unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be careful with their
words in unscripted settings. If the tone differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have reduced the pressure to make the set-piece

speeches more accessible. Research on central bank communication often uses measures such as
the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was
beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison suggests four speeches from 2026 (1, 2, 3,
4) were harder to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches scored in the 11 to 12
range, which is high-school-level complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and 15, which is

academic or technically dense text requiring university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-Governor Phil Lowe also scored better
on these metrics than the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that readability metrics do
not necessarily imply better clarity if the concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has

begun to put more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those invisible ‘star’ variables
(NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences
has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on the full range of transmission channels from
interest rates to inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour supply channel). This is

understandable in a world of supply shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt they would be comforted, though, by the
Deputy Governor’s explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the smallest of the
transmission channels from interest rates to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on inflation. It raises the question of whether

other policy tools or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on inflation through the other
channels without hitting a minority of households disproportionately.
We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases to summarise the position. From ‘narrow
path’ to ‘not ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once entrenched, a phrase can
outlast its usefulness.

### Page 4

Related articles
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record the presentations to the Board and the
members’ discussion. The minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-meeting
statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The
August 2024 minutes were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how sensitive the forecasts
were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed

Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s communication have not changed. It
wants to explain – and perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how it interprets
incoming data, especially inflation, the labour market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ about
future moves. Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular future path for interest

rates. Some academic literature argues that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high, for
that matter) independently amplifies the transmission of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-
period interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield curve. Central banks including the RBA
have certainly tried jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view, though, is that the
potential cost of reputational loss if things do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a

slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides the signal about future policy directions. This
is why it was noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour market as ‘a little tight’ after the
September meeting, even though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We have been
noting for some time that the RBA staff have a downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the

RBA’s oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not
surprise us.

台光電 2383 TT ( 電子–電子零組件 )

光耀群倫，電馭群雄 
投資與評價 
1. 台光電 3Q26 受惠在 GPU 新平台 M9 規格產品進入量產，營運將續創新
高；4Q26 各產品線有望調漲 5~20%不等，帶動台光電營運將續創新高。 
2. 26、27 年 GPU 及更多 ASIC 客戶都開始採用 M8+甚至 M9 CCL，高階板
材升級持續 ， 台光電也因應客戶需求逐年開出更多產能 ， 預期營運將連續創
高，預估 26 年 EPS 134.91 元、27 年 EPS 282.30 元。 
3. 考量台光電將持續受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，無論技術、產
能擴張幅度皆優於全球同業，26、27 年獲利將大幅走升，目標價上修至 7,00
元(25 x 27(F)PER)，並將投資評等由買進轉為強力買進。 
重點更新 
3Q26 台光電 M9 產品進入量產階段，營運創歷史新高 
台光電 3Q26 受惠於 GPU 客戶下一世代運算平台之 M9 產品正式量產 ， 應用於
UBB(HGX)、LPU 等高速傳輸板材中，大幅提升公司產品平均單價；ASIC 客戶
端需求持續加大，並且將板材全面升級至 M8+規格，高階 CCL 佔比持續擴大，
帶動台光電 3Q26 營收季增 28.8%達 608.85 億元，優於原估之 556.19 億元
(+17.6% QoQ)9.5% 。毛利率方面，公司持續將現有產能往高階產品推動，
M7/M8 等級以上 CCL 出貨佔比快速拉高，毛利率達 37.7%(+3.8 ppts QoQ)，
優於原先預期。且因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得宜，營業費用率為
7.0%，優於原預估 7.3%，帶動營業利益達 187.03 億元(+46.7% QoQ)，稅後
淨利季增 46.9%至 145.0 億元，EPS 40.47 元，獲利維持高增長且營運創新高。 
反映原物料持續緊缺之價格調漲，預期台光電 4Q26 營運將續創新高 
邁入 4Q26，預期台光電將持續受惠於各大 AI 客戶於新世代板材規格提升所帶
動的量價齊揚 ， 儘管年底台灣廠區擴廠進度將有所延誤 ， 惟中國崑山廠區亦將有
望提前上線彌補缺口，公司整體產能仍將維持穩定成長。此外，考量上游原材料
供給緊缺依舊，4Q26 台光電 CCL 各產品線有望再度進行 5~20%之價格調漲。
故上修台光電 4Q26 營收預估 18.0%至 721.20 億元(+18.5% QoQ)，並受益於
漲價的持續以及產品組合持續轉佳之下，毛利率預期將再提升 2.6 個百分點至
40.3%；考量產能擴充持續提升下，調高營業費用預估 16.4%至 51.93 億元
(+21.8% QoQ)，惟營業淨利仍將季增 27.8%至 238.76 億元，預估稅後淨利達
186.31 億元(+28.5% QoQ)，EPS 51.99 元，營運將持續創下歷史新高。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
3Q26 台光電 M9 產品進入量產階段，營運創歷史新高 
台光電 3Q26 受惠於 GPU 客戶下一世代運算平台之 M9 產品正式量產， 應用於UBB(HGX)、LPU
等高速傳輸板材中，大幅提升公司產品平均單價；ASIC 客戶端需求持續加大，並且將板材全面
升級至 M8+規格 ， 高階CCL 佔比持續擴大，帶動台光電 3Q26 營收季增 28.8%達 608.85 億元，
優於原估之 556.19 億元(+17.6% QoQ)9.5%。 
毛利率方面，公司持續將現有產能往高階產品推動，M7/M8 等級以上 CCL 出貨佔比快速拉高，
毛利率達 37.7%(+3.8 ppts QoQ)，優於原先預期。且因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得
宜，營業費用率為 7.0%，優於原預估 7.3%，帶動營業利益達 187.03 億元(+46.7% QoQ)，稅
後淨利季增 46.9%至 145.0 億元，EPS 40.47 元，獲利維持高速增長且營運達歷史新高。

反映原物料持續緊缺之價格調漲，預期台光電 4Q26 營運將續創新高 
邁入 4Q26，預期台光電將持續受惠於各大 AI 客戶於新世代板材規格提升所帶動的量價齊揚，
儘管年底台灣廠區擴廠進度將有所延誤，惟中國崑山廠區亦將有望提前上線彌補缺口，公司整體
產能仍將維持穩定成長。此外，考量上游原材料供給緊缺依舊，4Q26 台光電 CCL 各產品線有望
再度進行 5~20%之價格調漲。故上修台光電 4Q26 營收預估 18.0%至 721.20 億元(+18.5% 
QoQ)，並受益於漲價的持續以及產品組合持續轉佳之下，毛利率預期將再提升 2.6 個百分點至
40.3%；考量產能擴充持續提升下，調高營業費用預估 16.4%至 51.93 億元(+21.8% QoQ)，惟
營業淨利仍將季增 27.8%至 238.76 億元，預估稅後淨利達 186.31 億元(+28.5% QoQ)，EPS 
51.99 元，營運將持續創下歷史新高。

EPS 51.99  37.91  +37.2%  
資料來源: 國泰證期研究部整理

台光電將開始出貨 M9 CCL 產品，26 年營運創新高 
AI GPU、ASIC 伺服器新平台逐步放量 ， 台光電在技術領先優勢下為其中多家大廠的主要供應商 ，
其中除 25 年主要 ASIC 客戶外，今年另一家美系 ASIC 客戶需求倍數成長，其 CCL 規格要求也
由 M7 提升至 M8+，台光電將在日系同業產能不足情況下取得更高份額。此外 GPU 大客戶首度
採用 M9 規格之產品，將大幅提升單位產品價格，多數客戶訂單預期將持續強勁至年底，加上
1.6T Switch 預期也將於 4Q26 開始進入生產，研究部維持預期 26 年高階 CCL 營收佔比將達
75%不變。 
此外， 考量 4Q26 仍將進行價格調漲政策下 ，故上修整體營收預估 8.3%至 2,133.47 億元
(+126.3% YoY)。考量漲價效益以及產品組合持續優化，大幅調高毛利率預估 6.6 個百分點至
36.4%，並且受益於營運規模擴大與費用控管，調降營業費用率預估至 7.2%(-2.4 ppts YoY)，營
業利益為 624.24 億元(+226.7% YoY)，帶動稅後淨利預期將達 483.44 億元(+230.0% YoY)，優
於原估 408.57 億元之 18.3%，EPS 134.91 元，營運創新高。

CCL 高階升級趨勢不變且將開出更多產能，預期台光電 27 年營運再度創高 
展望 27 年，AI 相關需求在更高傳輸速度及訊號低損耗條件下，終端 PCB 普遍向 M8 以上規格
靠攏，同時板層數也持續往 30 甚至 40 層以上研發，帶動整體 CCL 價量齊揚。而繼GPU 客戶率
先採用 M9 CCL 產品，台光電兩大 ASIC 客戶亦預期將於 27 年導入 M9 規格於，以提升 AI 效能
之極致；衛星方面，台光電目前為地面板供應商之一，同時將進入下世代 v3 版本的天上衛星板
供應，供應 M8 等級 CCL。產能方面，儘管台灣廠區因土建、安規驗收等問題有所遞延，惟預期
截至 4Q27 仍將有 870 萬張月產能，相較 4Q26 將年增近五成，預期將滿足客戶未來新世代高
階 CCL 產品備貨需求，並使營運進一步擴大，維持產業高市佔之表現。

### Page 4

預期台光電在 M8+ CCL 以上領域仍為客戶供應商首選，加上產能進一步擴大開出下，上修 27
年整體營收預估 13.9%至 3,768.70 億元(+76.6% YoY)，伴隨 M9 出貨量持續放大，同時預期更
多終端應用將 M8+ CCL 作為主要板材選擇，加上產能緊缺與原料問題有望持續進行價格調整，
大幅上修毛利率預估 4.8 個百分點至 41.6%，且受益於營收規模持續大幅提升，調低營業費用率
至 6.9%(-0.3 ppts YoY)，預期營業利益為 1,296.54 億元(+107.7% YoY)，帶動稅後淨利年增
109.2%至 1,011.55 億元，EPS 282.30 元，獲利將翻倍成長且營運再度創高

技術、產能均穩居業界龍頭，台光電 26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升 
台光電 26 年於 AI、高階交換器，甚至衛星客戶需求強勁成長，以及漲價策略帶動下營運將季季
增，27 年持續維持領先技術為客戶 M9 CCL 首選，且大量新產能開出也將成為未來幾年台光電
在高階 CCL 市場中保持高市佔的關鍵，中長期營運持續成長，預期至27 年獲利仍保持年增超過
八成，預估 26 年 EPS 134.91 元、27 年 EPS 282.30 元。 
目前台光電股價為 5,025 元，約為 17.08 x FY27E EPS，位於近 3 年 PER 參考區間 10~30x 中間
偏下之位置。儘管目前於最高規格之 CCL 解方出現分歧，Q-Glass 石英布與 PTFE 等無布方案皆
受到高度討論，惟研究部認為製程良率、成本管控上將會是材料選用、量產之關鍵，且材料定案
時程仍尚需要一些時間，惟整體 CCL 產業材料持續升級趨勢不變下，在規格和用量上皆持續向
上，持續看好台光電於高階產品應用、客戶廣度，以及在現有、擴增產能規模上持續領先同業，
將有助公司持續維持高市佔之表現，26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升，調高 27 年獲利預估，並將
投資評等由買進轉為強力買進，目標價由 5,300 元(25x 修正前 27(F)PER)上修至 7,000 元(25x 修
正後 27(F)PER)。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年 3 月，為全球最大無鹵素 CCL 供應商，公司產品
主要包含：銅箔基板、黏合片與多 層壓合板等，以終端需求進行區分，3Q26 年手持式營收占比
10~15%，基礎建設營收占比 75~ 80%，車用和其他占 5~10%。

台光電 ESG 政策 
環境：台光電子秉持「環境保護與永續經營並重」的理念，將環境管理納入核心經營策略，並積
極推動  能源管理及碳排放管理。公司已於  2024 年成功將每單位銷售金額的能源消耗量較  
2023 年減少 23.01%， 並將溫室氣體排放總量降至 23,080.51 公噸 ， 減少了 29.70%的排放強
度。此外，台光 電子也已完成對大陸各廠區 ISO 50001 能源管理系統的驗證，並推動太陽能發
電項目 ， 進一步 提升綠能使用比例 。 原材料方面 ， 所有主要銅箔供應商均可提供 100%回收銅 ，
體現資源循環利 用的承諾。展望未來，台光電子以 2030 年減碳 30%為目標，並計劃於 2050 
年達成淨零排放， 持續致力於推動環境永續。  
社會： 台光電子認為員工是公司最重要的資產，致力於員工的多元共融與專業成長。公司在  
2024 年達 成了 85%員工年齡在 50 歲以下，且大力推動女性在管理層的參與，女性主管比例
達 10%以上。 此外，公司秉持員工健康與安全的優先理念，所有員工的健檢率達 100%，並提
供完善的職業安  全衛生管理。台光電子也積極參與社區建設，並在台灣廠區認養並維護超過  
300 平方米的綠地， 為員工與社區提供更綠意盎然的環境。 
公司治理： 台光電子始終秉持誠信、透明與問責的公司治理理念，並將永續發展納入董事會的
決策範疇 。 公 司治理架構中 ， 董事會設有專責的企業永續發展委員會 ， 負責審議並推動各項 ESG 
議題。台光電 子已成功納入「台灣永續指數」 ，並以透明的財務報告與風險管理策略，確保公司
治理與股東利 益一致。此外，公司也對供應鏈進行管理，確保其符合企業社會責任準則，並且
確保所有主要供 應商達到環保與社會責任的要求。  
永續供應鏈與長期展望： 台光電子在推動永續供應鏈管理上，已要求供應商遵守  RBA 行為準
則與衝突礦產規範，並且積 極實施供應商的環境與社會責任評估。隨著 5G 基站材料與車用電
子需求的增長，台光電子將持 續推動高附加價值產品，並以低碳製造與責任治理為長期發展重
心。公司已設定以 2023 年為基 準年，並計劃在 2030 年減碳 30%，在 2050 年達成淨零碳
排，致力於成為全球電子材料領域的 永續競爭者。

1
《升息不利地產REITs，但租賃期短者表現較佳》
利率變動會直接牽動房地產投資信託基金 (REITs)的
融資成本增加 ，壓縮可分配現金流與股利 ，依高盛

研究報告顯示，從2006年起的10年期美國國債收益
率上升至少40個基點的滾動六個月週期內，REITs的
表現比標普 500指數低 6.7%，過去五年 ，大多數
REIT 與 10 年期公債殖利率呈負相關，主要因為受
到租賃期間長短的影響 ，其中以飯店業及倉儲設施
因租賃期間較短可當下反應租金變動 ，購物中心因
經濟表現佳 ，可支配所得增加 ，所以表現反而較佳 ；
淨租賃、醫療保健REITs表現最差，但以近期而言，
業績 及營運模式的調整重要性也上升 ，醫療 保健
REITs受個別基本面不同，部分業者營運模式從過去
較長天期的淨租賃轉往養老住宅 ，縮短租賃期，老
年人口財富負擔能力提升，支撐老人住房市場定價

《預期地產交易價格降溫 ，4Q26交易量增長放緩 》

Green Street CPPI 數據顯示，全美商業地產價格
相較於低點已呈現微幅回升 ，8月上漲0.8%。過去
一年累計上漲5%，但美國國債收益率的上升及對借
貸成本的影響 ，恐促使購房者重新考慮支付意願 ，
預計漲勢即將降溫。依高盛統計截至8月的季度累計
地產交易量年增 18%(扣除合併 )，高於世邦魏理仕
和仲量聯行對 3Q26投資銷售額年增 13%和14%的
預期，8月份的強勁成長主要由工業地產和辦公地產
推動，而多戶住宅市場仍然落後 。工業倉儲主要受
惠供應過剩逐漸下降 ，租賃活動受到美國製造業回
流、近岸外包與第三方物流供應鏈重組支撐 ，優質
近港與門戶型物流中心空置率開始回落；辦公室物
業則呈現科技與專業服務企業偏好承租一線 A級商
辦，而不青睞 B/C級商辦 ，拆除舊型辦公室 、改建
住宅等 。多戶住宅仍在消化陽光帶 (德州、佛州等)
過剩的存量，壓抑短期租金漲幅 。受30年期固定房
貸利率在9月升至7%的高位及去年基期也高的影響 ，
預期4Q26交易量放緩。

《REITs股息吸引力低，轉注意現金流成長動能》
美REITs的回報在利率下降時會擴張估值，但現美國
10年期公債殖利率多 在5%以上，美國REITs平均股
息收益率約3.9%，相對不具投資吸引力 ，對房地產
持中立。然若考量租金現金流增長 ，租金定價力相
對強勢的族群主要為養老住宅 ，同店淨營利年增率
可達20%上下，但估值偏高，建議回檔可留意。

## Document 484: サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 14 日

1/2 
サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか 
～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

情報漏洩を中心にサイバー攻撃による被害が国内外で目立っている。相次ぐサイバー攻撃には
AIが悪用された可能性が指摘されており、今後もこうした被害は拡大する懸念がある。サイバー
攻撃による経済的な影響は主に3 つ考えられる。 
 まず、被害による直接的な金銭損失であり、仮想通貨等の資産の流出、身代金の支払い、被害
者への補償金支払いなどが挙げられる。FBIが被害申告ベースでまとめた「Annual Internet 
Crime Report 2025」によると、米国を中心としたサイバー犯罪の被害総額（個人被害も含む）は
209 億ドルと近年飛躍的に拡大している。なお、こうした被害は基本的に所得移転であり、生産活
動を捕捉する国内総生産（GDP）に直接的な影響を与えるわけではない。 
 次に、サイバー攻撃を通じた企業活動への損害だ。例えばCSISとMcAfeeによる 2020 年の調査で
は、サイバー攻撃を受けた場合に業務は平均18 時間中断したと指摘する。サイバー攻撃は既存顧
客へのサービス提供や新たな顧客獲得活動などを抑制する懸念がある。これに加えて、企業のブ
ランド価値を毀損し、顧客は該当サービスの利用を当面ためらうかもしれない。 
 サイバー攻撃による経済活動への被害が一企業に留まるのであれば、競合他社が代替的にサー
ビスを提供する、或いはシステム復旧後に挽回生産などが生じることで、マクロ的な影響は限定
される。しかし、こうした被害が「交通インフラなど代替が難しい企業」、或いは「産業内の広範
な企業」に及ぶ場合、サイバー攻撃で損なわれた供給能力を補うことが難しく、マクロ経済全体
の損失へと波及する。AIを通じたサイバー攻撃の頻発は、従来のミクロ的な「一企業の被害」か
ら、マクロ的な「産業や経済全体の被害」に転じるリスクを高めている可能性がある。 
 最後に、サイバーセキュリティ対策の強化によるIT関連支出の増加であり、これはGDPを押し上
げる可能性が高い。例えば、2000 年問題（西暦が2000 年になるとコンピュータが誤作動を起こす
との懸念）の際のIT対策費用は全世界で3,000～6,000 億ドルと、1999 年における世界GDPの 0.9
～1.8%に達したとみられている。一方、攻撃をうける企業の防御対策よりも、AI脅威論による開
発抑制の動きが強まる場合、データセンター投資や株式市場の調整を通じて景気減速へと繋がる
懸念がある。なお、米国ではトランプ政権が対中競争力の観点からAI規制に消極的であるほか、
連邦議会におけるFrontier ActなどのAI規制法案も成立に至るかは不透明だ。

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 28 日

1/3 
タイ中銀、インフレ加速も景気支援を重視して４会合連続で金利据え置き 
～原油高やバーツ安など物価上昇リスクがくすぶるなか、難しい政策運営が続くであろう～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ タイ銀行（中央銀行）は、10 月 28 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利
を４会合連続で1.00％に据え置くことを決定した。 同国では、 イラン情勢の悪化による原油
高などを背景にインフレが加速しているものの、内需の弱さを反映して需要面からの物価上
昇圧力は限定的とみられる。 
○ 一方で、世界的なＡＩ関連需要の拡大が一部産業を押し上げているものの、家計債務の大き
さや雇用環境の脆弱さなどが個人消費の重しとなり、景気回復の恩恵は一部にとどまってい
るとの見方を示している。さらに、原油高による対外収支の悪化や洪水対策に伴う財政負担
の増大、通貨バーツ安なども景気や物価に対する不透明感を強めている。 
○ こうしたなか、中銀は足元の金利水準が景気回復を支えるうえで適切との認識を示し、イン
フレの上昇は一時的との見方を維持するなど、景気支援を優先する姿勢を示した。ただし、
エルニーニョ現象やイラン情勢を巡る不透明感に加え、金融緩和の長期化に伴うリスクも意
識されるなか、先行きの政策運営は引き続き難しい局面が続くと見込まれる。

タイ銀行（中央銀行）は、10 月 28 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利を４
会合連続で1.00％に据え置くことを決定した。同行は、 イラン情勢が緊迫化する直前に開催した２
月会合において、同国経済のコロナ禍からの回復が ASEAN（東南アジア諸国連合）主要国のなかで
も遅れていることに加え、 物価の低迷が続いていることなどを背景に利下げに踏み切った。しかし、
直後にイラン情勢が緊迫化したことで状況は一変した。その結果、 中銀は金利を据え置く様子見姿
勢を維持している。

### Page 2

2/3 
タイでは、2025 年半ば以降のインフレ率はマイナスで推移していたものの、2026 年４月以降は一
転してプラスに転じている。 直近９月の
インフレ率は前年同月比＋ 2.82％と中
銀の目標レンジ （２±１％） 内にとどま
るものの、 上限に近付くなどインフレ圧
力が強まっている様子がうかがえる （図
１） 。コアインフレ率は前年同月比＋
1.50％にとどまるものの、2023 年５月以
来の高い伸びとなるなど、 インフレの動
きが広がりをみせている可能性がある。
とはいえ、 コアインフレ率は依然として
低水準にあり、 需要面からの物価上昇圧
力も限定的とみられることが、中銀の様子見姿勢を後押ししたと考えられる。 
金融市場においては、原油高が対外収支を悪化させるとともに、政府による家計支援を目的とし
た補助金政策などに伴う財政悪化への懸念も重なり、経済のファンダメンタルズ（基礎的条件）の
脆弱化が警戒されてきた。さらに、タイでは９月中旬以降、豪雨による洪水被害が広がり、政府は
洪水対策を目的 に財政 支援を拡充し た
ため、 公的債務残高のＧＤＰ比は法定上
限（70％） に近付いている （注１） 。 こう
した事情も重なり、 通貨バーツ相場は調
整の動きを強めて いる（図２） 。タイで
は、伝統的に金取引が活発であり、バー
ツ相場は 年明け直後にかけて金価格に
連れ高する形で強含んだ。しかし、足元
では金価格が調整の動きを強めており、
こうした動きも バーツ安を後押しして
いる可能性がある。バーツ安は輸出競争
力を高める一方、 輸入物価の上昇を通じてインフレ圧力を増幅させることが懸念される。 とはいえ、
足元では景気の不透明要因が山積しており、中銀にとっては様子見姿勢を維持せざるを得ない事情
もうかがえる 
会合後に公表した声明文では、今回の決定が「全会一致（出席した６名の政策委員が据え置きを
支持し、 １名が欠席）」 であったことを明らかにした。 タイ経済については 「ＡＩ （人工知能） 関連
の好循環が景気の勢いを増している」 ものの、 「経済成長率は潜在成長率を下回り、 景気拡大の恩恵

注１ 10 月２日付レポート「タイ、洪水被害が景気の新たな重石に」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666229.html）

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
は一部に集中している」との認識を示した。一方で、物価動向については「供給要因を理由に一時
的な上昇が見込まれる」 とした。 信用動向については 「全体的な伸びは加速している」 ものの、 「中
小企業向け融資は引き続き縮小している」ほか、 「中小企業や家計向け融資の質を注視する必要が
ある」との認識を示した。そのうえで、中銀の政策運営について「緩和的な金融政策と的を絞った
金融措置は景気回復を下支えしている」 と評価しつつ、 「金利を据え置き、 イラン情勢やエルニーニ
ョ現象によるインフレリスクの影響を注視する必要がある」とした。
具体的には、 「2026 年の経済成長率は＋2.4％、2027 年は＋2.1％になる」 との見通しを示す一方、

「その恩恵はＡＩに関連する製造業やサービス業の企業・労働者に集中している」とした。個人消
費については「家計債務の大きさ、脆弱な雇用環境、低所得世帯を中心とする購買力の弱さが重し
になっている」との見方を示した。加えて、「原油高が外国人観光客数の足かせになる」としたほ
か、「全体としての経済成長が潜在成長率を下回るなかで、中小企業は依然事業環境への適応や競
争激化という困難に直面している」との認識を示した。
物価動向については「2026 年のインフレ率は＋2.1％、2027 年は＋1.3％になる」との見通しを
示し、 「エネルギー価格やエルニーニョ現象の影響で 2027 年初めにかけて上昇が続く」一方、 「そ
の後は構造的な制約要因による低成長や内需の弱さを反映して鈍化する」 とした。 コアインフレ率
も「2026 年は＋1.2％、2027 年は＋1.3％になる」との見通しを示すとともに、 「中期的なインフレ
期待は依然として目標レンジ内に定着している」 とした。 ただし、 「イラン情勢の動向、 補助金財政

の余力低下、 企業によるコスト転嫁の影響、 中期的なインフレ期待による上振れリスクを注視する」
とした。
バーツ相場については「ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制度理事会）による引き締め観測や原油高が重しに
なっている」 とした。 一方で、 「国際的な金利上昇の動きにあわせてタイでも長期金利が上昇してい
る」ものの、 「民間部門の借入コストは概ね安定している」ほか、「大企業向けをけん引役に信用動
向は回復しているが、 中小企業向け融資は縮小している」、「中小企業や低所得者層をはじめ家計の
債務返済能力を注視する必要がある」との認識を示した。
先行きの政策運営については「物価安定と持続可能な経済成長、金融安定の維持を目指す」との
従来からの考え方を繰り返した。 そのうえで、 「足元の金利水準が景気回復を支えるうえで適切」と
して、景気支援を優先する考えを示した。物価動向については「供給要因による物価は上昇してい
るが、 今後もインフレ見通しや関連するリスクを注視する」 と８月の前回会合と同じ文言を繰り返

すなど、インフレが一時的なものにとどまるとの認識を維持している。一方、今回は「低金利が長
期的なマクロ金融安定に及ぼす影響」 にも言及するなど、 金融緩和の長期化に伴うリスクにも一定
の警戒姿勢を示した。とはいえ、 先行きはエルニーニョ現象の影響が長期化する懸念が高まってお
り、 世界経済や国際金融市場を巡る不透明要因も山積するなか、 政策の舵取りは引き続き難しい局
面が続くと考えられる。
以 上
